+Raises _Http4xxError for permanent 4xx errors.

+    Raises OSError for network errors (caller handles retries).

diff --git a/docs/design/FL_Architecture_Design.md b/docs/design/FL_Architecture_Design.md
index 04e0848a..7377fcfb 100644
--- a/docs/design/FL_Architecture_Design.md
+++ b/docs/design/FL_Architecture_Design.md
@@ -310,11 +310,11 @@ A FaultHandler is a **declarative definition** of a failure analysis and remedia
 | **AgentTask** (default) | Task description, skill descriptions, input declarations, secret references and agent (Cline, Claude Code, etc.) | Agent runtime (Cline, Claude Code or whatever agent is available), MCP integration, execution environment | Low-code/no-code handlers; most common path                                  |
 | **CustomRuntime** | Container image with `/analyze` entrypoint, fault-handler.yaml | Execution environment, FL SDK access | Advanced handlers needing custom logic, dependencies, or non-agent workflows |
 
-In case of AgentTask, FL provides the container that calls the AI Central APIs to execute a coding agent task. Currently only Cline is available as coding agent, but in future there will be other agents like Claude Code. 
+In case of AgentTask, FL provides the container that calls the AI Central APIs to execute a coding agent task. Currently only Cline is available as coding agent, but in future there will be other agents like Claude Code.
 The AI Central API will schedule an Atom on Doit, which will execute the actual coding agent.
 This will bring some latency as the scheduling itself could take several minutes, but as the analysis are performed offline this shouldn't be a big problem.
-AI Central should be scalable enough for the purpose of FL as each coding agent execution happens in its own Atom and Doit has enough resources. 
-In case of CustomRuntime the contributors provide their own container (image) that will be run as k8s job to perform the analysis. This custom analysis could do some pre-post processing and call AI Central (via sdk from FL) to delegate some part of the analysis to coding agent or it could call SAP AI core directly. It is up to the contributor to decide. The exact interface is to be defined. 
+AI Central should be scalable enough for the purpose of FL as each coding agent execution happens in its own Atom and Doit has enough resources.
+In case of CustomRuntime the contributors provide their own container (image) that will be run as k8s job to perform the analysis. This custom analysis could do some pre-post processing and call AI Central (via sdk from FL) to delegate some part of the analysis to coding agent or it could call SAP AI core directly. It is up to the contributor to decide. The exact interface is to be defined.
 
 Both models break down into four concrete sub-cases depending on who contributes the handler:

+

@@ -12,6 +16,7 @@ class RawClineTaskItem(BaseModel):
         tokens_in: Prompt tokens consumed by the agent. None if the SDK omits the field.
         tokens_out: Completion tokens produced by the agent. None if the SDK omits the field.
     """
+
     tokens_in: int | None = Field(None, alias="tokensIn")

@@ -28,6 +33,7 @@ class RawClineState(BaseModel):

     model_config = {"populate_by_name": True}
@@ -42,10 +48,12 @@ class RawTrajectory(BaseModel):
         cline_state: JSON-encoded ``RawClineState``. None when the SDK omits the field.

     """
+
     cline_state: str | None = None
     mlflow_run_id: str | None = None
 
     def parse_cline_state(self) -> RawClineState:
+        """Parse the JSON-encoded cline_state field into a RawClineState model."""
         return RawClineState.model_validate_json(self.cline_state or "{}")
 
 
@@ -58,6 +66,7 @@ class AgentTaskRequest(BaseModel):
             Used as the key when looking up trajectories after the Doit atom completes.

@@ -98,6 +107,7 @@ class AgentTaskResult(BaseModel):

     """
+
     task_id: str
     completion_text: str | None = None
     repo_diff: str | None = None

diff --git a/fl_control_plane/data_extractor/config.py b/fl_control_plane/data_extractor/config.py
index b0f08c74..cb8e2569 100644
--- a/fl_control_plane/data_extractor/config.py
+++ b/fl_control_plane/data_extractor/config.py
@@ -11,6 +11,8 @@
 
 from __future__ import annotations
 
+import ssl
+
 from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict
 
 
@@ -33,9 +35,25 @@ class DataExtractorSettings(BaseSettings):
     """
 
     model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
-        env_file=None,        # production reads from os.environ; .env is loaded by python-dotenv at app startup
+        env_file=None,  # production reads from os.environ; .env is loaded by python-dotenv at app startup
         case_sensitive=False,
         extra="ignore",
     )
 
     requests_ca_bundle: str | None = None
+
+
+def build_ssl_context() -> ssl.SSLContext | bool:
+    """Return SSLContext from CA bundle setting, or True to use system trust store.
+
+    When ``REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE`` is set, builds an ``ssl.SSLContext`` pinned to
+    that PEM file. When absent, returns ``True`` so aiohttp uses the default
+    system trust store — the SLES container image includes the SAP CA bundle,
+    so Jenkins TLS verification works without explicit configuration in
+    production.
+    """
+    ca_bundle = DataExtractorSettings().requests_ca_bundle
+    if ca_bundle:
+        return ssl.create_default_context(cafile=ca_bundle)
+    # SLES container image includes SAP CA bundle in system trust store
+    return True

+# Downloader orchestration coordinates multiple independent services
+# and synchronization primitives. Argument count reflects explicit
+# dependency passing rather than a missing abstraction.
+# pylint: disable=too-many-arguments,too-many-positional-arguments
+async def _process_in_memory_fetch(
+    item: CaptureItem,
+    hdlf_path: str,
+    data: bytes,
+    head_cap: int | None,
+    config: DownloaderConfig,
+    lock: asyncio.Lock,
+    inspection_bytes: list[int],
+    hdlf_client: HdlfClient,
+) -> DownloadResult:
+    """Upload a small in-memory fetch result to HDLF, applying cap and truncation."""
+    if item.skip_if_empty_json_array and data.strip() == b"[]":
+        return DownloadResult(key=item.key, status=ItemStatus.CAPTURED, captured_size=0)
+    original_size = len(data)
+    captured_size = original_size
+    if original_size > 1024 * 1024:
+        log.info("downloader: fetched %s (%d bytes)", item.key, original_size)
+    # Stage logs under _STAGE_LOG_CAP fit in memory and are never
+    # truncated — append marker only when cap was hit exactly.
+    if head_cap is not None and original_size >= head_cap:
+        marker = _HEAD_TRUNCATED_MARKER.format(cap_mb=head_cap // (1024 * 1024)).encode()
+        data = data + b"\n" + marker
+        captured_size = len(data)
+        final_status: ItemStatus = ItemStatus.TRUNCATED
+    else:
+        final_status = ItemStatus.CAPTURED
+    async with lock:
+        if inspection_bytes[0] + captured_size > config.per_inspection_cap:
+            return DownloadResult(key=item.key, status=ItemStatus.SKIPPED_INSPECTION_CAP)
+        inspection_bytes[0] += captured_size
+    try:
+        await hdlf_client.put_object(hdlf_path, data)
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return DownloadResult(
+            key=item.key,
+            status=ItemStatus.SKIPPED_UNREACHABLE,
+            error=str(exc),
+        )
+    except Exception as exc:
+        log.exception("downloader: unexpected error writing %s: %s", hdlf_path, exc)
+        raise
+    log.info("downloader: done %s (%s, %d bytes)", item.key, final_status.value, captured_size)
+    return DownloadResult(
+        key=item.key,
+        status=final_status,
+        original_size=original_size,
+        captured_size=captured_size,
+    )
+
+
+# Downloader orchestration coordinates multiple independent services
+# and synchronization primitives. Argument count reflects explicit
+# dependency passing rather than a missing abstraction.
+# pylint: disable=too-many-arguments,too-many-positional-arguments,too-many-locals
+async def _process_disk_fetch(
+    item: CaptureItem,
+    hdlf_path: str,
+    tmp_path: str,
+    original_size: int,
+    console_head_size: int | None,
+    head_cap: int | None,
+    config: DownloaderConfig,
+    lock: asyncio.Lock,
+    inspection_bytes: list[int],
+    hdlf_client: HdlfClient,
+) -> DownloadResult:
+    """Upload a large disk-buffered fetch result to HDLF, applying cap and truncation."""
+    if original_size > 1024 * 1024:
+        log.info("downloader: fetched %s (%d bytes)", item.key, original_size)
+    reported_size = console_head_size if console_head_size is not None else original_size
+
+    if head_cap is not None and original_size >= head_cap:
+        # Stage log hit the head cap mid-stream: append the truncation
+        # marker to the temp file in a thread (avoids blocking the loop).
+        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
+        await loop.run_in_executor(
+            None,
+            _append_head_truncation_marker,
+            tmp_path,
+            head_cap,
+            config,
+        )
+        final_status = ItemStatus.TRUNCATED
+    elif item.is_console_log and reported_size > config.per_file_cap:
+        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
+        truncated_path = await loop.run_in_executor(
+            None,
+            _truncate_console_log,
+            tmp_path,
+            reported_size,
+            config,
+        )
+        _cleanup(tmp_path)
+        tmp_path = truncated_path
+        final_status = ItemStatus.TRUNCATED
+    else:
+        final_status = ItemStatus.CAPTURED
+
+    captured_size = os.path.getsize(tmp_path)
+    async with lock:
+        if inspection_bytes[0] + captured_size > config.per_inspection_cap:
+            _cleanup(tmp_path)
+            return DownloadResult(key=item.key, status=ItemStatus.SKIPPED_INSPECTION_CAP)
+        inspection_bytes[0] += captured_size
+    try:
+        await hdlf_client.put_file_atomic(hdlf_path, tmp_path)
+    except OSError as exc:
+        _cleanup(tmp_path)
+        return DownloadResult(
+            key=item.key,
+            status=ItemStatus.SKIPPED_UNREACHABLE,
+            error=str(exc),
+        )
+    except Exception as exc:
+        _cleanup(tmp_path)
+        log.exception("downloader: unexpected error writing %s: %s", hdlf_path, exc)
+        raise
+    _cleanup(tmp_path)
+    log.info("downloader: done %s (%s, %d bytes)", item.key, final_status.value, captured_size)
+    return DownloadResult(
+        key=item.key,
+        status=final_status,
+        original_size=reported_size,
+        captured_size=captured_size,
+    )
+
+
+# Downloader orchestration coordinates multiple independent services
+# and synchronization primitives. Argument count reflects explicit
+# dependency passing rather than a missing abstraction.
+# pylint: disable=too-many-arguments,too-many-positional-arguments
+async def _try_ranged_console_fetch(
+    item: CaptureItem,
+    hdlf_path: str,
+    config: DownloaderConfig,
+    session: aiohttp.ClientSession,
+    auth_header: str | None,
+    lock: asyncio.Lock,
+    inspection_bytes: list[int],
+    hdlf_client: HdlfClient,
+) -> tuple[DownloadResult | None, int | None]:
+    """Probe console log size and attempt a ranged fetch for large logs.
+
+    Returns (DownloadResult, None) if the ranged path handled the item.
+    Returns (None, console_head_size) to signal the caller should proceed
+    with the standard retry loop (console_head_size may be None).
+    """
+    if not (item.is_console_log and item.node_steps_url is None):
+        return None, None
+
+    console_head_size: int | None = await _head_content_length(item.source_url, session, auth_header)
+    if console_head_size is None:
+        console_head_size = await _range_probe(item.source_url, session, auth_header)
+    if console_head_size is not None:
+        log.info("downloader: console log %s is %d bytes", item.key, console_head_size)
+
+    if console_head_size is None or console_head_size <= config.per_file_cap:
+        return None, console_head_size
+
+    truncated = await _fetch_console_log_ranged(item.source_url, console_head_size, config, session, auth_header)
+    if truncated is None:
+        log.warning("downloader: range fetch failed for %s, falling back to full download", item.key)
+        return None, console_head_size
+
+    captured_size = len(truncated)
+    async with lock:
+        if inspection_bytes[0] + captured_size > config.per_inspection_cap:
+            return DownloadResult(key=item.key, status=ItemStatus.SKIPPED_INSPECTION_CAP), None
+        inspection_bytes[0] += captured_size
+    try:
+        await hdlf_client.put_object(hdlf_path, truncated)
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return DownloadResult(key=item.key, status=ItemStatus.SKIPPED_UNREACHABLE, error=str(exc)), None
+    except Exception as exc:
+        log.exception("downloader: unexpected error writing %s: %s", hdlf_path, exc)
+        raise
+    log.info("downloader: done %s (truncated via range, %d bytes)", item.key, captured_size)
+    return DownloadResult(
+        key=item.key,
+        status=ItemStatus.TRUNCATED,
+        original_size=console_head_size,
+        captured_size=captured_size,
+    ), None
+
+
+# pylint: disable=too-many-arguments,too-many-positional-arguments,too-many-locals,too-many-return-statements,too-many-branches

+# Downloader orchestration coordinates multiple independent services
+# and synchronization primitives. Argument count reflects explicit
+# dependency passing rather than a missing abstraction.
+# pylint: disable=too-many-arguments,too-many-positional-arguments,too-many-locals
+async def _upload_step_logs(
+    folder: str,
+    item: CaptureItem,
+    step_log_bodies: list[tuple[BlueOceanStep, bytes]],
+    stage_prefix: str,
+    hdlf_client: HdlfClient,
+    config: DownloaderConfig,
+    lock: asyncio.Lock,
+    inspection_bytes: list[int],
+) -> tuple[int, DownloadResult | None]:
+    """Upload individual step log files to HDLF, checking caps.
+
+    Iterates over step_log_bodies, writing each entry as a separate
+    `{stage_prefix}/steps/{step_id}.log` file.  Uses put_object for bodies
+    below _STEP_SMALL_CAP and put_file_atomic (via a temp file) for larger
+    ones.  Stops and returns early if the per-inspection cap is hit or an
+    OSError occurs.
+
+    Returns:
+        (total_bytes_uploaded, None) on success.
+        (0, DownloadResult(SKIPPED_UNREACHABLE)) on OSError.
+    """
+    total_captured = 0
+    for step, body in step_log_bodies:
+        step_key = f"{stage_prefix}/steps/{step.id}.log"
+        hdlf_path = f"{folder}/{step_key}"
+        async with lock:
+            if inspection_bytes[0] + len(body) > config.per_inspection_cap:
+                break
+            inspection_bytes[0] += len(body)
+        try:
+            if len(body) < _STEP_SMALL_CAP:
+                await hdlf_client.put_object(hdlf_path, body)
+            else:
+                tmp_fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=config.tmpdir, suffix=".step")
+                try:
+                    with os.fdopen(tmp_fd, "wb") as f:
+                        f.write(body)
+                    await hdlf_client.put_file_atomic(hdlf_path, tmp_path)
+                finally:
+                    _cleanup(tmp_path)
+            total_captured += len(body)
+        except OSError as exc:
+            log.warning("downloader: put_object failed for %s: %s", step_key, exc)
+            return 0, DownloadResult(
+                key=item.key,
+                status=ItemStatus.SKIPPED_UNREACHABLE,
+                error=str(exc),
+            )
+        except Exception as exc:
+            log.exception("downloader: unexpected error writing step log %s: %s", step_key, exc)
+            raise
+    return total_captured, None
+
+

+# and synchronization primitives. Argument count reflects explicit
+# dependency passing rather than a missing abstraction.

+def _extract_git_repos(api: RawBuildResponse) -> list[GitRepoInfo]:
+    """Build GitRepoInfo list from /api/json SCM actions."""
+    git_repos = []

+        if action.last_built_revision and action.last_built_revision.branch:
+            branch = action.last_built_revision.branch[0]
+            git_repos.append(
+                GitRepoInfo(
+                    repo_url=action.remote_urls[0] if action.remote_urls else "",
+                    commit=branch.SHA1,

+                )

+
+
+def _extract_pr_info(blue: RawBlueRunResponse, git_repos: list[GitRepoInfo]) -> PrInfo | None:
+    """Derive PrInfo from Blue Ocean run response, falling back to git branch names."""
+    if blue.pull_request and blue.pull_request.id:
+        pr_url = blue.pull_request.url or (blue.branch.url if blue.branch else None)
+        return PrInfo(
+            pr_number=int(blue.pull_request.id),
+            pr_title=blue.pull_request.title or "",
+            pr_author=blue.pull_request.author or "",
+            pr_url=pr_url or "",
+        )
+
+    pr_match = re.match(r"PR-(\d+)$", blue.pipeline or "")
+    if pr_match:
+        pr_url = blue.branch.url if blue.branch else None
+        return PrInfo(
+            pr_number=int(pr_match.group(1)),
+            pr_title="",
+            pr_author="",
+            pr_url=pr_url or "",
+        )
+
+    for repo in git_repos:
+        m = re.search(r"(?:refs/pull/|PR-)(\d+)", repo.branch)
+        if m:
+            return PrInfo(
+                pr_number=int(m.group(1)),
+                pr_title="",
+                pr_author="",
+                pr_url="",
+            )
+
+    return None
+
+

+                        message=item.msg[:80],
+                        affected_paths=item.affected_paths[:10],

@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+"""Shared helpers for gate implementations."""
+
+from fl_control_plane.pipeline_analyzer.models import GateVerdict
+
+
+def continue_verdict(gate_id: str) -> GateVerdict:
+    """Return a standard CONTINUE verdict with no message or findings."""
+    return GateVerdict(
+        gate_id=gate_id,
+        analysis_decision="CONTINUE",
+        require_finalizer=False,
+        message=None,
+    )

diff --git a/fl_control_plane/temporal/_activity_helpers.py b/fl_control_plane/temporal/_activity_helpers.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..0afa5d5c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/fl_control_plane/temporal/_activity_helpers.py
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+"""Shared helpers for Temporal activity implementations."""
+
+from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
+from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
+from typing import NoReturn
+
+from temporalio.exceptions import ApplicationError
+
+from fl_shared.hdlf_client import HdlfClient, HdlfConfigurationError
+from fl_shared.hdlf_client.config import Settings as HdlfSettings
+
+
+def raise_non_retryable(exc: Exception) -> NoReturn:
+    """Re-raise exc as a non-retryable ApplicationError with InvalidInput type."""
+    raise ApplicationError(str(exc), type="InvalidInput", non_retryable=True) from exc
+
+
+@asynccontextmanager
+async def open_hdlf_client() -> AsyncIterator[HdlfClient]:
+    """Create and yield an HdlfClient configured from HdlfSettings.
+
+    Raises:
+        ApplicationError: Non-retryable (``type="InvalidInput"``) when mTLS
+            cert material is missing — a deployment/config problem that a
+            Temporal retry cannot recover from.
+    """
+    _hdlf_cfg = HdlfSettings()
+    if _hdlf_cfg.hdlf_rest_api_host is None or _hdlf_cfg.hdlf_container_id is None:
+        raise ApplicationError(
+            "HDLF_REST_API_HOST and HDLF_CONTAINER_ID must be set",
+            type="InvalidInput",
+            non_retryable=True,
+        )
+    try:
+        hdlf_cm = HdlfClient(
+            _hdlf_cfg.hdlf_rest_api_host,
+            _hdlf_cfg.hdlf_container_id,
+            cert_dir=_hdlf_cfg.hdlf_cert_dir,
+            client_certificate=_hdlf_cfg.hdlf_client_certificate,
+            client_key=_hdlf_cfg.hdlf_client_key,
+        )
+    except HdlfConfigurationError as exc:
+        raise ApplicationError(
+            f"HDLF client configuration error: {exc}",
+            type="InvalidInput",
+            non_retryable=True,
+        ) from exc
+    async with hdlf_cm as hdlf:
+        yield hdlf

 class CircuitBreaker:
     """Exponential backoff circuit breaker for the reconcile loop.
+
     After `failure_threshold` consecutive failures the circuit opens and imposes
     exponential backoff before allowing the next attempt. A single success resets
     the breaker to closed.
@@ -23,6 +24,7 @@ def __init__(
         base_backoff_seconds: float = _DEFAULT_BASE_BACKOFF_SECONDS,
         max_backoff_seconds: float = _DEFAULT_MAX_BACKOFF_SECONDS,
     ) -> None:
+        """Initialize with failure threshold and backoff parameters."""
         self._failure_threshold = failure_threshold
         self._base_backoff_seconds = base_backoff_seconds
         self._max_backoff_seconds = max_backoff_seconds
@@ -61,12 +63,13 @@ def record_failure(self) -> None:
 
     def backoff_seconds(self) -> float:
         """Compute the current backoff duration based on failure count.
+
         Returns 0 if the circuit is closed (below threshold).
         """
         if not self.is_open:
             return 0.0
         exponent = self._consecutive_failures - self._failure_threshold
-        backoff = self._base_backoff_seconds * (2**exponent)
+        backoff = self._base_backoff_seconds * float(2**exponent)
         return min(backoff, self._max_backoff_seconds)
 
     def should_allow_request(self) -> bool:
@@ -81,4 +84,4 @@ def should_allow_request(self) -> bool:
     def reset(self) -> None:
         """Force-reset the breaker to closed state (used in tests)."""
         self._consecutive_failures = 0

-    from fastapi import FastAPI
+class _RelistManager:
+    """Manages the background relist task and its stop event as a unit."""
 
-    from .endpoints import router
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self.task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None
+        self.stop_event: asyncio.Event | None = None
 
+    def start(self) -> None:
+        """Create stop event and launch the relist loop. Guards against double-start."""
+        if self.task is not None and not self.task.done():
+            return
+        self.stop_event = asyncio.Event()
+        self.task = asyncio.create_task(_relist_loop(self.stop_event))
+
+    async def stop(self) -> None:
+        """Signal the relist loop to stop, wait for it, then reset state.
+
+        Primary shutdown: cooperative via stop_event.set() — the loop checks
+        the event between cycles and exits cleanly.
+        Fallback: task.cancel() wakes the loop immediately if it is blocked
+        on a long wait_for() call (e.g. slow circuit-breaker backoff).
+        """
+        if self.stop_event is not None:
+            self.stop_event.set()
+        if self.task is not None:
+            self.task.cancel()
+            try:
+                await self.task
+            except asyncio.CancelledError:
+                pass
+            finally:
+                self.task = None
+                self.stop_event = None
+
+
+_relist_manager = _RelistManager()
+
+

+            is_active=False,
+            has_error=True,

diff --git a/hdlf_server/_percent_decode.py b/hdlf_server/_percent_decode.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..9b0fe58b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hdlf_server/_percent_decode.py
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+"""Percent-decoding utility shared by reader and handler_results_router."""
+
+from urllib.parse import unquote
+
+from hdlf_server.exceptions import InvalidPathError
+
+# Iteration cap for the fixed-point unquote loop.
+# Real attacker payloads nest at most one or two layers; the cap rejects
+# pathological inputs without admitting untrusted state.
+_MAX_DECODE_ITERATIONS = 8
+
+
+def percent_decode_fixed_point(value: str, label: str = "value") -> str:
+    """Repeatedly percent-decode value until stable.
+
+    Strategy: percent-decode to a fixed point so any chain of encodings
+    collapses to its final form. A single ``unquote`` only undoes one
+    layer — ``%252e%252e`` would decode to ``%2e%2e`` and pass a ``..``
+    check. The fixed-point loop catches arbitrary nesting
+    (``%25252e`` → ``%252e`` → ``%2e`` → ``.``).
+
+    Args:
+        value: The percent-encoded string to decode.
+        label: Name used in the error message, e.g. ``"subpath"`` or
+            ``"fault_id"``.
+
+    Returns:
+        The fully-decoded string.
+
+    Raises:
+        InvalidPathError: ``value`` still changes after
+            ``_MAX_DECODE_ITERATIONS`` passes — pathological or malicious
+            input rejected rather than admitted as untrusted state.
+    """
+    decoded = value
+    for _ in range(_MAX_DECODE_ITERATIONS):
+        once = unquote(decoded)
+        if once == decoded:
+            break
+        decoded = once
+    else:
+        raise InvalidPathError(f"{label} has too many percent-encoding layers")
+    return decoded

     """The inspection folder for the requested UUID does not exist in HDLF."""
 
     def __init__(self, inspection_uuid: str):

         super().__init__(f"Inspection {inspection_uuid!r} not found")
         self.inspection_uuid = inspection_uuid
 
@@ -28,9 +29,8 @@ class InspectionFileNotFoundError(HdlfServerError):
     """
 
     def __init__(self, inspection_uuid: str, relative_path: str):
-        super().__init__(
-            f"Inspection {inspection_uuid!r}: file {relative_path!r} not found"
-        )
+        """Initialize with the inspection UUID and the missing file path."""
+        super().__init__(f"Inspection {inspection_uuid!r}: file {relative_path!r} not found")
         self.inspection_uuid = inspection_uuid
         self.relative_path = relative_path

diff --git a/tests/data_extractor/test_downloader_coverage.py b/tests/data_extractor/test_downloader_coverage.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..7f8252ad
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/data_extractor/test_downloader_coverage.py
@@ -0,0 +1,426 @@
+"""Tests for downloader.py — error paths, cap logic, and pure utility functions."""
+
+import asyncio
+import os
+import tempfile
+from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch
+
+import aiohttp
+import pytest
+
+from fl_control_plane.data_extractor import (
+    CaptureItem,
+    DownloaderConfig,
+    ItemStatus,
+)
+from fl_control_plane.data_extractor.downloader import (
+    _CONSOLE_HEAD,
+    _CONSOLE_TAIL,
+    _append_head_truncation_marker,
+    _backoff,
+    _cleanup,
+    _fetch_console_log_ranged,
+    _head_content_length,
+    _parse_content_range_total,
+    _process_in_memory_fetch,
+    _range_probe,
+    _RetryError,
+    _run_url_and_node_id_from_steps_url,
+)
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Helpers
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _cfg(**kwargs) -> DownloaderConfig:
+    defaults: dict = {
+        "per_file_cap": 10 * 1024 * 1024,
+        "per_inspection_cap": 100 * 1024 * 1024,
+        "max_retries": 3,
+        "max_concurrency": 4,
+        "max_step_concurrency": 4,
+        "tmpdir": tempfile.gettempdir(),
+    }
+    defaults.update(kwargs)
+    return DownloaderConfig(**defaults)
+
+
+def _item(key: str = "test/item.log", **kwargs) -> CaptureItem:
+    defaults: dict = {
+        "key": key,
+        "source_url": "https://jenkins.example.com/log",
+        "is_console_log": False,
+        "skip_if_empty_json_array": False,
+    }
+    defaults.update(kwargs)
+    return CaptureItem(**defaults)
+
+
+def _mock_hdlf() -> AsyncMock:
+    client = AsyncMock()
+    client.put_object = AsyncMock()
+    return client
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _parse_content_range_total
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestParseContentRangeTotal:
+    def test_parses_standard_header(self) -> None:
+        """Standard Content-Range header returns correct total."""
+        assert _parse_content_range_total("bytes 0-0/108456789") == 108456789
+
+    def test_returns_none_for_empty_string(self) -> None:
+        """Empty string returns None."""
+        assert _parse_content_range_total("") is None
+
+    def test_returns_none_for_star_total(self) -> None:
+        """bytes 0-0/* (unknown total) returns None via ValueError."""
+        assert _parse_content_range_total("bytes 0-0/*") is None
+
+    def test_returns_none_for_malformed(self) -> None:
+        """Malformed header returns None."""
+        assert _parse_content_range_total("not-a-range") is None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _run_url_and_node_id_from_steps_url
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestRunUrlAndNodeIdFromStepsUrl:
+    def test_parses_valid_steps_url(self) -> None:
+        """Returns (run_url, node_id) for well-formed Blue Ocean steps URL."""
+        url = "https://jenkins.example.com/blue/rest/organizations/jenkins/pipelines/my-job/runs/42/nodes/5/steps/"
+        result = _run_url_and_node_id_from_steps_url(url)
+        assert result is not None
+        run_url, node_id = result
+        assert node_id == "5"
+        assert run_url.endswith("/42")
+
+    def test_returns_none_for_missing_nodes_segment(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when URL does not contain /nodes/{id}/steps/."""
+        assert _run_url_and_node_id_from_steps_url("https://jenkins.example.com/other/path") is None
+
+    def test_returns_none_for_non_numeric_node_id(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when node id is not numeric."""
+        assert _run_url_and_node_id_from_steps_url("https://j.example.com/runs/1/nodes/abc/steps/") is None
+
+    def test_accepts_url_without_trailing_slash(self) -> None:
+        """Accepts URLs without trailing slash on /steps."""
+        url = "https://j.example.com/runs/1/nodes/7/steps"
+        result = _run_url_and_node_id_from_steps_url(url)
+        assert result is not None
+        assert result[1] == "7"
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _backoff
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestBackoff:
+    def test_returns_positive_value(self) -> None:
+        """Backoff is always positive."""
+        for attempt in range(5):
+            assert _backoff(attempt) > 0
+
+    def test_increases_with_attempt(self) -> None:
+        """Later attempts have higher average backoff (statistical test over many samples)."""
+        import statistics
+
+        samples_0 = [_backoff(0) for _ in range(50)]
+        samples_3 = [_backoff(3) for _ in range(50)]
+        assert statistics.mean(samples_3) > statistics.mean(samples_0)
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _cleanup
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestCleanup:
+    def test_removes_existing_file(self, tmp_path) -> None:
+        """Existing file is deleted without error."""
+        f = tmp_path / "tmp.log"
+        f.write_bytes(b"data")
+        _cleanup(str(f))
+        assert not f.exists()
+
+    def test_no_error_for_nonexistent_path(self) -> None:
+        """No exception when path does not exist."""
+        _cleanup("/tmp/nonexistent_fl_test_file_xyz.tmp")
+
+    def test_no_error_on_oserror(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
+        """OSError during unlink is silently swallowed."""
+        f = tmp_path / "locked.log"
+        f.write_bytes(b"x")
+        monkeypatch.setattr(os, "unlink", lambda p: (_ for _ in ()).throw(OSError("locked")))
+        _cleanup(str(f))  # should not raise
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _append_head_truncation_marker
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestAppendHeadTruncationMarker:
+    def test_appends_marker_with_correct_mb(self, tmp_path) -> None:
+        """Marker includes the correct cap size in MB."""
+        f = tmp_path / "stage.log"
+        f.write_bytes(b"log content")
+        cap = 5 * 1024 * 1024  # 5 MB
+        _append_head_truncation_marker(str(f), cap, _cfg())
+        content = f.read_bytes()
+        assert b"5" in content
+        assert b"log content" in content  # original content preserved
+
+    def test_marker_appended_to_existing_content(self, tmp_path) -> None:
+        """Marker is appended, not replacing existing content."""
+        f = tmp_path / "log.txt"
+        f.write_bytes(b"existing")
+        _append_head_truncation_marker(str(f), 1024 * 1024, _cfg())
+        content = f.read_bytes()
+        assert content.startswith(b"existing")
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _process_in_memory_fetch
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestProcessInMemoryFetch:
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_skips_empty_json_array(self) -> None:
+        """Returns CAPTURED with size 0 when data is '[]' and skip flag is set."""
+        item = _item(skip_if_empty_json_array=True)
+        hdlf = _mock_hdlf()
+        lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        result = await _process_in_memory_fetch(item, "path/key.json", b"[]", None, _cfg(), lock, [0], hdlf)
+        assert result.status == ItemStatus.CAPTURED
+        assert result.captured_size == 0
+        hdlf.put_object.assert_not_called()
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_skips_empty_json_array_with_whitespace(self) -> None:
+        """Whitespace-padded '[]' is also skipped."""
+        item = _item(skip_if_empty_json_array=True)
+        hdlf = _mock_hdlf()
+        lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        result = await _process_in_memory_fetch(item, "path/key.json", b"  []  ", None, _cfg(), lock, [0], hdlf)
+        assert result.status == ItemStatus.CAPTURED
+        assert result.captured_size == 0
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_non_empty_json_array_not_skipped(self) -> None:
+        """Non-empty array is uploaded normally."""
+        item = _item(skip_if_empty_json_array=True)
+        hdlf = _mock_hdlf()
+        lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        await _process_in_memory_fetch(item, "path/key.json", b'[{"id":1}]', None, _cfg(), lock, [0], hdlf)
+        hdlf.put_object.assert_called_once()
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_inspection_cap_blocks_upload(self) -> None:
+        """Returns SKIPPED_INSPECTION_CAP when adding this item would exceed the cap."""
+        item = _item()
+        hdlf = _mock_hdlf()
+        lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        cfg = _cfg(per_inspection_cap=10)
+        inspection_bytes = [5]  # already 5 bytes used; data is 10 bytes → would exceed
+        result = await _process_in_memory_fetch(
+            item, "path/key.log", b"0123456789", None, cfg, lock, inspection_bytes, hdlf
+        )
+        assert result.status == ItemStatus.SKIPPED_INSPECTION_CAP
+        hdlf.put_object.assert_not_called()
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_head_cap_appends_truncation_marker(self) -> None:
+        """When data length >= head_cap, truncation marker is appended and status is TRUNCATED."""
+        item = _item()
+        hdlf = _mock_hdlf()
+        lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        data = b"x" * (5 * 1024 * 1024)
+        head_cap = 5 * 1024 * 1024
+        result = await _process_in_memory_fetch(item, "path/key.log", data, head_cap, _cfg(), lock, [0], hdlf)
+        assert result.status == ItemStatus.TRUNCATED
+        hdlf.put_object.assert_called_once()
+        written = hdlf.put_object.call_args[0][1]
+        assert b"5" in written  # marker mentions 5 MB
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_hdlf_oserror_returns_skipped_unreachable(self) -> None:
+        """OSError from hdlf.put_object returns SKIPPED_UNREACHABLE."""
+        item = _item()
+        hdlf = _mock_hdlf()
+        hdlf.put_object = AsyncMock(side_effect=OSError("connection refused"))
+        lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        result = await _process_in_memory_fetch(item, "path/key.log", b"data", None, _cfg(), lock, [0], hdlf)
+        assert result.status == ItemStatus.SKIPPED_UNREACHABLE
+        assert "connection refused" in (result.error or "")
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _fetch_console_log_ranged
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestFetchConsoleLogRanged:
+    def _mock_session(self, head_data: bytes, tail_data: bytes) -> MagicMock:
+        """Build a session mock that returns head_data and tail_data for Range GETs."""
+
+        async def _fake_range_get(url, headers, timeout, allow_redirects):
+            resp = AsyncMock()
+            resp.status = 206
+            resp.read = AsyncMock(return_value=head_data if "0-" in headers.get("Range", "") else tail_data)
+            resp.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+            resp.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+            return resp
+
+        session = MagicMock()
+        session.get = _fake_range_get
+        return session
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_none_when_file_fits_in_head_plus_tail(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when total_size <= CONSOLE_HEAD + CONSOLE_TAIL (no truncation needed)."""
+        session = MagicMock()
+        result = await _fetch_console_log_ranged(
+            "https://j.ex/log", _CONSOLE_HEAD + _CONSOLE_TAIL - 1, _cfg(), session, None
+        )
+        assert result is None
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_omit_marker_contains_correct_mb(self) -> None:
+        """Assembled result contains omission marker with correct MB count."""
+        total = _CONSOLE_HEAD + _CONSOLE_TAIL + 100 * 1024 * 1024  # 100 MB omitted
+        head_bytes = b"H" * _CONSOLE_HEAD
+        tail_bytes = b"T" * _CONSOLE_TAIL
+
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.data_extractor.downloader._range_get", new_callable=AsyncMock) as mock_rg:
+            mock_rg.side_effect = [head_bytes, tail_bytes]
+            result = await _fetch_console_log_ranged("https://j.ex/log", total, _cfg(), MagicMock(), None)
+
+        assert result is not None
+        assert b"100" in result  # marker mentions 100 MB omitted
+        assert result.startswith(head_bytes[:10])
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_none_on_network_error(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when Range GET raises aiohttp.ClientError (fallback to full download)."""
+        with patch(
+            "fl_control_plane.data_extractor.downloader._range_get",
+            side_effect=aiohttp.ClientError("timeout"),
+        ):
+            result = await _fetch_console_log_ranged(
+                "https://j.ex/log",
+                _CONSOLE_HEAD + _CONSOLE_TAIL + 1024,
+                _cfg(),
+                MagicMock(),
+                None,
+            )
+        assert result is None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _head_content_length and _range_probe (via mock session)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestHeadContentLength:
+    def _mock_head_session(self, status: int, content_length: str | None) -> MagicMock:
+        resp = AsyncMock()
+        resp.status = status
+        resp.headers = {"Content-Length": content_length} if content_length else {}
+        resp.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+        resp.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+        session = MagicMock()
+        session.head = MagicMock(return_value=resp)
+        return session
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_content_length_on_200(self) -> None:
+        """Returns parsed int when server responds 200 with Content-Length."""
+        session = self._mock_head_session(200, "12345")
+        result = await _head_content_length("https://j.ex/log", session, None)
+        assert result == 12345
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_none_on_non_200(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None for non-200 responses."""
+        session = self._mock_head_session(404, None)
+        result = await _head_content_length("https://j.ex/log", session, None)
+        assert result is None
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_none_when_no_content_length(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when 200 response has no Content-Length header."""
+        session = self._mock_head_session(200, None)
+        result = await _head_content_length("https://j.ex/log", session, None)
+        assert result is None
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_none_on_client_error(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when aiohttp.ClientError is raised."""
+        session = MagicMock()
+        session.head = MagicMock(side_effect=aiohttp.ClientError("timeout"))
+        result = await _head_content_length("https://j.ex/log", session, None)
+        assert result is None
+
+
+class TestRangeProbe:
+    def _mock_range_session(self, status: int, content_range: str | None) -> MagicMock:
+        resp = AsyncMock()
+        resp.status = status
+        resp.headers = {"Content-Range": content_range} if content_range else {}
+        resp.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+        resp.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+        session = MagicMock()
+        session.get = MagicMock(return_value=resp)
+        return session
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_total_size_from_content_range(self) -> None:
+        """Returns total size from Content-Range header on 206 response."""
+        session = self._mock_range_session(206, "bytes 0-0/987654")
+        result = await _range_probe("https://j.ex/log", session, None)
+        assert result == 987654
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_none_for_non_206(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when server returns anything other than 206."""
+        session = self._mock_range_session(200, None)
+        result = await _range_probe("https://j.ex/log", session, None)
+        assert result is None
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_none_on_client_error(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when aiohttp.ClientError is raised."""
+        session = MagicMock()
+        session.get = MagicMock(side_effect=aiohttp.ClientError("timeout"))
+        result = await _range_probe("https://j.ex/log", session, None)
+        assert result is None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# _RetryError
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestRetryError:
+    def test_stores_suggested_delay(self) -> None:
+        """suggested_delay attribute is accessible."""
+        exc = _RetryError("HTTP 429", suggested_delay=30.0)
+        assert exc.suggested_delay == 30.0
+
+    def test_none_delay_by_default(self) -> None:
+        """suggested_delay is None when not provided."""
+        exc = _RetryError("HTTP 503")
+        assert exc.suggested_delay is None
+
+    def test_is_io_error(self) -> None:
+        """_RetryError is an IOError subclass."""
+        assert isinstance(_RetryError("x"), IOError)

+
+
+async def test_list_handler_results_skips_fault_with_invalid_json(hdlf_client: MagicMock) -> None:
+    """A fault folder whose result.json contains invalid JSON is silently skipped."""
+    hdlf_client.list_dir = AsyncMock(return_value=[_dir_entry("insp/handler_results/bad-fault")])
+
+    async def fake_get_object(path: str) -> bytes:
+        if path.endswith("result.json"):
+            return b"not valid json {"
+        raise FileNotFoundError(path)
+
+    hdlf_client.get_object = fake_get_object
+    hdlf_client.exists = AsyncMock(return_value=True)
+
+    async with HdlfInspectionResultsRepository(hdlf_client) as repo:
+        entries = await repo.list_handler_results("insp")
+
+    assert entries == []
+
+
+async def test_list_handler_results_treats_unrecognised_scope_as_none(hdlf_client: MagicMock) -> None:
+    """A fault folder with an unrecognised scope value in metadata returns scope=None."""
+    hdlf_client.list_dir = AsyncMock(return_value=[_dir_entry("insp/handler_results/fault-x")])
+    metadata_json = {"scope": "something_unexpected", "addressed_stages": []}
+
+    async def fake_get_object(path: str) -> bytes:
+        if path.endswith("result.json"):
+            return b'{"issues": []}'
+        if path.endswith("result_description.md"):
+            return b""
+        if path.endswith("handler_metadata.json"):
+            return json.dumps(metadata_json).encode()
+        raise FileNotFoundError(path)
+
+    hdlf_client.get_object = fake_get_object
+    hdlf_client.exists = AsyncMock(return_value=True)
+
+    async with HdlfInspectionResultsRepository(hdlf_client) as repo:
+        entries = await repo.list_handler_results("insp")
+
+    assert len(entries) == 1
+    assert entries[0].scope is None
+
+
+async def test_write_handler_result_wraps_runtime_error_as_configuration_error(hdlf_client: MagicMock) -> None:
+    """RuntimeError from the HDLF client (e.g. used outside context manager) is wrapped as ConfigurationError."""
+    hdlf_client.put_object_atomic = AsyncMock(side_effect=RuntimeError("client not open"))
+
+    async with HdlfInspectionResultsRepository(hdlf_client) as repo:
+        with pytest.raises(InspectionResultsConfigurationError):
+            await repo.write_handler_result(
+                inspection_id="insp-1",
+                fault_id="sonarqube-failure",
+                result_json={"issues": []},
+                code_changes=None,
+                output_description=None,
+                scope=None,
+                addressed_stages=None,
+            )
+
+
+async def test_list_handler_results_wraps_runtime_error_as_configuration_error(hdlf_client: MagicMock) -> None:
+    """RuntimeError from list_dir (e.g. session closed) is wrapped as ConfigurationError."""
+    hdlf_client.list_dir = AsyncMock(side_effect=RuntimeError("session closed"))
+
+    async with HdlfInspectionResultsRepository(hdlf_client) as repo:
+        with pytest.raises(InspectionResultsConfigurationError):
+            await repo.list_handler_results("insp-1")
+
+
+async def test_read_code_changes_wraps_runtime_error_as_configuration_error(hdlf_client: MagicMock) -> None:
+    """RuntimeError from get_object is wrapped as ConfigurationError."""
+    hdlf_client.get_object = AsyncMock(side_effect=RuntimeError("not initialised"))
+
+    async with HdlfInspectionResultsRepository(hdlf_client) as repo:
+        with pytest.raises(InspectionResultsConfigurationError):
+            await repo.read_code_changes("insp-1", "sonarqube-failure")

--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/mcp_servers/generic/_shared.py
@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+"""Shared test helpers for the generic MCP server tests."""
+
+from unittest.mock import AsyncMock
+
+
+def fake_reader(**method_mocks: AsyncMock) -> AsyncMock:
+    """Return a fake InspectionReader usable as an async context manager."""
+    reader = AsyncMock()
+    reader.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=reader)
+    reader.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=None)
+    for name, mock in method_mocks.items():
+        setattr(reader, name, mock)
+    return reader

+@pytest.mark.asyncio

+    """scan_pr_data escalates medium-confidence to AI and returns the AI result."""

+        reason="AI determined this is WIP",
+        matched_rule="ai_review_title_keywords",
+        confidence=ConfidenceLevel.HIGH,
+        explanation="AI determined this is WIP",
+        fix_suggestion="Remove WIP prefix",
+        ai_enhanced=True,
+    )
+
+    detector = WipDetector(enable_ai_review=True)

+        result = await detector.scan_pr_data(_pr_data(title="WIP: feature"))
+
+    assert result.ai_enhanced is True
+    assert result.confidence == ConfidenceLevel.HIGH
+
+

+    """scan_pr_data returns rule-based result when AI review raises WipDetectionError."""
+    detector = WipDetector(enable_ai_review=True)
+    with patch.object(detector, "_ai_wip_review", new=AsyncMock(side_effect=WipDetectionError("AI unavailable"))):
+        result = await detector.scan_pr_data(_pr_data(title="WIP: feature"))
+
+    assert result.ai_enhanced is False
+    assert result.matched_rule == "title_keywords"

diff --git a/tests/test_ai_hub.py b/tests/test_ai_hub.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..fad566df
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_ai_hub.py
@@ -0,0 +1,470 @@
+"""Unit tests for fl_control_plane/ai_hub.py — pure logic, no LLM calls."""
+
+from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import BaseModel
+
+from fl_control_plane.ai_hub import (
+    FaultLocalizationLLMError,
+    FaultLocalizationRefusalError,
+    FaultLocalizationValidationError,
+    _extract_and_parse_json,
+    _extract_json_from_llm_response,
+    _format_validation_feedback,
+    _get_plain_text_refusal_message,
+    _get_structured_refusal_message,
+    _has_json_structure,
+    _inject_schema_into_messages,
+    _inline_defs,
+    sap_ai_hub_chat,
+    sap_ai_hub_chat_structured,
+)
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Exception initialisation
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestFaultLocalizationLLMError:
+    def test_default_usage_when_none_passed(self) -> None:
+        """usage defaults to a copy of _EMPTY_USAGE when not provided."""
+        exc = FaultLocalizationLLMError("boom")
+        assert exc.usage["prompt_tokens"] == 0
+        assert exc.usage["model_name"] == ""
+
+    def test_usage_is_copied(self) -> None:
+        """Mutations to the returned usage dict do not affect other instances."""
+        exc1 = FaultLocalizationLLMError("a")
+        exc2 = FaultLocalizationLLMError("b")
+        exc1.usage["prompt_tokens"] = 99
+        assert exc2.usage["prompt_tokens"] == 0
+
+    def test_explicit_usage_stored(self) -> None:
+        """Provided usage dict is stored as-is."""
+        usage = {"model_name": "gpt-4", "prompt_tokens": 10, "completion_tokens": 5, "total_tokens": 15}
+        exc = FaultLocalizationLLMError("msg", usage=usage)
+        assert exc.usage["model_name"] == "gpt-4"
+
+
+class TestFaultLocalizationRefusalError:
+    def test_stores_refusal_reason(self) -> None:
+        """refusal_reason attribute is accessible after construction."""
+        exc = FaultLocalizationRefusalError("msg", "content policy violated")
+        assert exc.refusal_reason == "content policy violated"
+
+    def test_default_usage(self) -> None:
+        """usage defaults to empty usage when not provided."""
+        exc = FaultLocalizationRefusalError("msg", "reason")
+        assert exc.usage["total_tokens"] == 0
+
+    def test_explicit_usage(self) -> None:
+        """Provided usage is stored."""
+        usage = {"model_name": "x", "prompt_tokens": 1, "completion_tokens": 2, "total_tokens": 3}
+        exc = FaultLocalizationRefusalError("msg", "reason", usage=usage)
+        assert exc.usage["total_tokens"] == 3
+
+
+class TestFaultLocalizationValidationError:
+    def test_stores_validation_errors(self) -> None:
+        """validation_errors list is accessible after construction."""
+        exc = FaultLocalizationValidationError("msg", ["field x: missing"])
+        assert exc.validation_errors == ["field x: missing"]
+
+    def test_default_usage(self) -> None:
+        """usage defaults to empty usage when not provided."""
+        exc = FaultLocalizationValidationError("msg", [])
+        assert exc.usage["completion_tokens"] == 0
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# JSON extraction helpers
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestExtractJsonFromLlmResponse:
+    def test_extracts_json_from_surrounding_text(self) -> None:
+        """JSON object extracted from text with surrounding prose."""
+        result = _extract_json_from_llm_response('Here is the result: {"key": "value"} done.')
+        assert result == '{"key": "value"}'
+
+    def test_returns_original_when_no_braces(self) -> None:
+        """Original text returned unchanged when no braces found."""
+        text = "no json here"
+        assert _extract_json_from_llm_response(text) == text
+
+    def test_returns_original_when_end_before_start(self) -> None:
+        """Original text returned when closing brace precedes opening brace."""
+        text = "} something { "
+        assert _extract_json_from_llm_response(text) == text
+
+
+class TestHasJsonStructure:
+    def test_true_for_valid_json_object(self) -> None:
+        """Returns True when text contains matching braces."""
+        assert _has_json_structure('{"a": 1}') is True
+
+    def test_false_for_no_braces(self) -> None:
+        """Returns False when text has no braces."""
+        assert _has_json_structure("plain text") is False
+
+    def test_false_when_closing_before_opening(self) -> None:
+        """Returns False when } comes before {."""
+        assert _has_json_structure("} text {") is False
+
+
+class TestExtractAndParseJson:
+    def test_parses_valid_json(self) -> None:
+        """Valid JSON object string is parsed to dict."""
+        result = _extract_and_parse_json('{"is_wip": true}')
+        assert result == {"is_wip": True}
+
+    def test_raises_value_error_for_no_json(self) -> None:
+        """ValueError raised when no JSON structure in response."""
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not contain valid JSON"):
+            _extract_and_parse_json("no braces at all")
+
+    def test_result_filter_applied_before_parse(self) -> None:
+        """result_filter is called on the response before JSON parsing."""
+        raw = 'PREFIX::{"x": 1}'
+        result = _extract_and_parse_json(raw, result_filter=lambda s: s.split("::", 1)[1])
+        assert result == {"x": 1}
+
+    def test_result_filter_bypasses_json_structure_check(self) -> None:
+        """When result_filter is provided, no JSON structure check is performed."""
+        # filter returns valid JSON; the original string has no braces — without
+        # the filter it would raise ValueError
+        result = _extract_and_parse_json("no braces", result_filter=lambda _: '{"k": "v"}')
+        assert result == {"k": "v"}
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Refusal detection
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestGetPlainTextRefusalMessage:
+    def test_detects_refusal_keyword_in_plain_text(self) -> None:
+        """Refusal message returned when keyword present and no JSON structure."""
+        msg = _get_plain_text_refusal_message("I cannot help with that.")
+        assert msg is not None
+        assert "cannot" in msg.lower()
+
+    def test_returns_none_when_json_structure_present(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when refusal keyword appears alongside JSON structure."""
+        msg = _get_plain_text_refusal_message('I cannot: {"result": "ok"}')
+        assert msg is None
+
+    def test_returns_none_when_no_refusal_keyword(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None for plain text with no refusal keywords."""
+        assert _get_plain_text_refusal_message("The answer is 42.") is None
+
+    def test_truncated_to_max_length(self) -> None:
+        """Returned message is truncated to MAX_REFUSAL_MESSAGE_LENGTH."""
+        long_text = "I cannot " + "x" * 1000
+        msg = _get_plain_text_refusal_message(long_text)
+        assert msg is not None
+        assert len(msg) <= 500
+
+
+class TestGetStructuredRefusalMessage:
+    def test_detects_refusal_field(self) -> None:
+        """Returns refusal string when 'refusal' key is set."""
+        msg = _get_structured_refusal_message({"refusal": "content policy"})
+        assert msg == "content policy"
+
+    def test_detects_content_filter_finish_reason(self) -> None:
+        """Returns message for content_filter finish_reason."""
+        msg = _get_structured_refusal_message({"finish_reason": "content_filter"})
+        assert msg is not None
+        assert "content_filter" in msg
+
+    def test_detects_content_filtered_finish_reason(self) -> None:
+        """Returns message for content_filtered finish_reason."""
+        msg = _get_structured_refusal_message({"finish_reason": "content_filtered"})
+        assert msg is not None
+
+    def test_detects_bedrock_stop_reason(self) -> None:
+        """Returns message when stopReason is content_filtered."""
+        msg = _get_structured_refusal_message({"stopReason": "content_filtered"})
+        assert msg == "Content filtered by AWS Bedrock"
+
+    def test_detects_error_field_with_refusal_keyword(self) -> None:
+        """Returns error string when error field contains a refusal keyword."""
+        msg = _get_structured_refusal_message({"error": "I cannot process this request"})
+        assert msg is not None
+
+    def test_returns_none_for_clean_response(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None for a clean JSON response with no refusal signals."""
+        assert _get_structured_refusal_message({"is_wip": False, "reason": "looks good"}) is None
+
+    def test_returns_none_for_error_without_refusal_keyword(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None when error field exists but contains no refusal keywords."""
+        assert _get_structured_refusal_message({"error": "timeout"}) is None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Schema helpers
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestInlineDefs:
+    def test_inlines_ref_in_properties(self) -> None:
+        """$ref in properties is replaced with the definition content."""
+        schema = {
+            "$defs": {"MyEnum": {"type": "string", "enum": ["a", "b"]}},
+            "properties": {"status": {"$ref": "#/$defs/MyEnum"}},
+        }
+        _inline_defs(schema)
+        assert schema["properties"]["status"] == {"type": "string", "enum": ["a", "b"]}
+        assert "$defs" not in schema
+
+    def test_no_op_when_no_defs(self) -> None:
+        """Schema is unchanged when no $defs section present."""
+        schema: dict = {"properties": {"x": {"type": "integer"}}}
+        _inline_defs(schema)
+        assert schema == {"properties": {"x": {"type": "integer"}}}
+
+    def test_inlines_ref_in_list(self) -> None:
+        """$ref inside a list item is resolved."""
+        schema = {
+            "$defs": {"Tag": {"type": "string"}},
+            "properties": {"tags": {"type": "array", "items": {"$ref": "#/$defs/Tag"}}},
+        }
+        _inline_defs(schema)
+        assert schema["properties"]["tags"]["items"] == {"type": "string"}
+
+    def test_unknown_ref_left_intact(self) -> None:
+        """$ref pointing outside $defs is left as-is."""
+        schema = {
+            "$defs": {},
+            "properties": {"x": {"$ref": "#/other/Thing"}},
+        }
+        _inline_defs(schema)
+        assert schema["properties"]["x"] == {"$ref": "#/other/Thing"}
+
+
+class TestInjectSchemaIntoMessages:
+    def test_appends_schema_to_system_message(self) -> None:
+        """Schema instruction is appended to existing system message content."""
+
+        class MyModel(BaseModel):
+            value: int
+
+        messages = [
+            {"role": "system", "content": "You are helpful."},
+            {"role": "user", "content": "Analyse this."},
+        ]
+        result = _inject_schema_into_messages(messages, MyModel)
+        system_content = next(m["content"] for m in result if m["role"] == "system")
+        assert "value" in system_content
+        assert "JSON" in system_content
+
+    def test_inserts_system_message_when_absent(self) -> None:
+        """A system message is created when none exists in messages."""
+
+        class MyModel(BaseModel):
+            flag: bool
+
+        messages = [{"role": "user", "content": "Go."}]
+        result = _inject_schema_into_messages(messages, MyModel)
+        assert result[0]["role"] == "system"
+        assert "flag" in result[0]["content"]
+
+    def test_original_messages_not_mutated(self) -> None:
+        """Input messages list is not modified in-place."""
+
+        class MyModel(BaseModel):
+            x: str
+
+        messages = [{"role": "system", "content": "original"}]
+        _inject_schema_into_messages(messages, MyModel)
+        assert messages[0]["content"] == "original"
+
+
+class TestFormatValidationFeedback:
+    def test_includes_field_errors(self) -> None:
+        """Output mentions the failing field name."""
+
+        class MyModel(BaseModel):
+            count: int
+
+        from pydantic import ValidationError as PydanticValidationError
+
+        feedback = ""
+        try:
+            MyModel.model_validate({"count": "not-a-number"})
+        except PydanticValidationError as exc:
+            feedback = _format_validation_feedback('{"count": "not-a-number"}', exc, MyModel)
+
+        assert "count" in feedback
+        assert "corrected" in feedback.lower()
+
+    def test_truncates_long_raw_response(self) -> None:
+        """Raw response in feedback is truncated to MAX_VALIDATION_RESPONSE_LENGTH."""
+
+        class MyModel(BaseModel):
+            x: int
+
+        from pydantic import ValidationError as PydanticValidationError
+
+        feedback = ""
+        try:
+            MyModel.model_validate({})
+        except PydanticValidationError as exc:
+            long_raw = "x" * 1000
+            feedback = _format_validation_feedback(long_raw, exc, MyModel)
+
+        assert "..." in feedback
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# sap_ai_hub_chat — integration of helpers
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestSapAiHubChat:
+    def _mock_raw(self, text: str) -> dict:
+        return {
+            "response_text": text,
+            "usage": {"model_name": "gpt-4", "prompt_tokens": 1, "completion_tokens": 1, "total_tokens": 2},
+        }
+
+    def test_returns_parsed_json_with_usage(self) -> None:
+        """Valid JSON response is parsed and usage key is included."""
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="gpt-4"),
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub._send_chat_to_openai", return_value=self._mock_raw('{"result": "ok"}')),
+        ):
+            result = sap_ai_hub_chat([{"role": "user", "content": "go"}])
+        assert result["result"] == "ok"
+        assert "usage" in result
+
+    def test_raises_refusal_error_on_plain_text_refusal(self) -> None:
+        """FaultLocalizationRefusalError raised when response is a plain-text refusal."""
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="gpt-4"),
+            patch(
+                "fl_control_plane.ai_hub._send_chat_to_openai", return_value=self._mock_raw("I cannot help with this.")
+            ),
+        ):
+            with pytest.raises(FaultLocalizationRefusalError):
+                sap_ai_hub_chat([{"role": "user", "content": "go"}])
+
+    def test_raises_llm_error_on_invalid_json(self) -> None:
+        """FaultLocalizationLLMError raised when response has no valid JSON."""
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="gpt-4"),
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub._send_chat_to_openai", return_value=self._mock_raw("not json at all")),
+        ):
+            with pytest.raises(FaultLocalizationLLMError):
+                sap_ai_hub_chat([{"role": "user", "content": "go"}])
+
+    def test_raises_refusal_error_on_structured_refusal(self) -> None:
+        """FaultLocalizationRefusalError raised when parsed JSON signals content_filter."""
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="gpt-4"),
+            patch(
+                "fl_control_plane.ai_hub._send_chat_to_openai",
+                return_value=self._mock_raw('{"finish_reason": "content_filter"}'),
+            ),
+        ):
+            with pytest.raises(FaultLocalizationRefusalError):
+                sap_ai_hub_chat([{"role": "user", "content": "go"}])
+
+    def test_routes_claude_model_to_aws(self) -> None:
+        """Models with 'claude' in name are routed to _send_chat_to_aws."""
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="claude-3"),
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub._send_chat_to_aws", return_value=self._mock_raw('{"x": 1}')) as mock_aws,
+        ):
+            sap_ai_hub_chat([{"role": "user", "content": "go"}])
+        mock_aws.assert_called_once()
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# sap_ai_hub_chat_structured
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class _SimpleModel(BaseModel):
+    value: int
+
+
+class TestSapAiHubChatStructured:
+    def _mock_chat(self, responses: list[dict]) -> MagicMock:
+        """Return a mock for sap_ai_hub_chat that returns responses in sequence."""
+        mock = MagicMock(side_effect=responses)
+        return mock
+
+    def test_returns_validated_model_on_first_try(self) -> None:
+        """Returns validated instance and usage when first response is valid."""
+        response = {
+            "value": 42,
+            "usage": {"model_name": "gpt-4", "prompt_tokens": 1, "completion_tokens": 1, "total_tokens": 2},
+        }
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="gpt-4"),
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.sap_ai_hub_chat", return_value=response.copy()),
+        ):
+            result, usage = sap_ai_hub_chat_structured([{"role": "user", "content": "go"}], _SimpleModel)
+        assert isinstance(result, _SimpleModel)
+        assert result.value == 42
+        assert "model_name" in usage
+
+    def test_raises_validation_error_after_retries_exhausted(self) -> None:
+        """FaultLocalizationValidationError raised when all retries fail validation."""
+        bad_response = {"value": "not-an-int", "usage": {}}
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="gpt-4"),
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.sap_ai_hub_chat", return_value=bad_response),
+        ):
+            with pytest.raises(FaultLocalizationValidationError) as exc_info:
+                sap_ai_hub_chat_structured([{"role": "user", "content": "go"}], _SimpleModel, validation_retries=1)
+        assert exc_info.value.validation_errors
+
+    def test_retries_with_feedback_on_validation_failure(self) -> None:
+        """sap_ai_hub_chat is called extra times when validation fails and retries remain."""
+        bad = {"value": "bad", "usage": {}}
+        good = {
+            "value": 7,
+            "usage": {"model_name": "gpt-4", "prompt_tokens": 1, "completion_tokens": 1, "total_tokens": 2},
+        }
+        call_count = 0
+
+        def _side_effect(messages, result_filter=None):
+            nonlocal call_count
+            call_count += 1
+            return good.copy() if call_count > 1 else bad.copy()
+
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="gpt-4"),
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.sap_ai_hub_chat", side_effect=_side_effect),
+        ):
+            result, _ = sap_ai_hub_chat_structured(
+                [{"role": "user", "content": "go"}], _SimpleModel, validation_retries=2
+            )
+        assert result.value == 7
+        assert call_count == 2
+
+    def test_skips_schema_injection_when_flag_false(self) -> None:
+        """Schema is not injected when include_schema_in_prompt=False."""
+        response = {"value": 1, "usage": {}}
+        captured_messages = []
+
+        def _capture(messages, result_filter=None):
+            captured_messages.extend(messages)
+            return response.copy()
+
+        with (
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.settings", sap_ai_hub_model_name="gpt-4"),
+            patch("fl_control_plane.ai_hub.sap_ai_hub_chat", side_effect=_capture),
+        ):
+            sap_ai_hub_chat_structured(
+                [{"role": "system", "content": "be terse"}, {"role": "user", "content": "go"}],
+                _SimpleModel,
+                include_schema_in_prompt=False,
+            )
+        system_msg = next((m for m in captured_messages if m["role"] == "system"), None)
+        assert system_msg is not None
+        assert "JSON" not in system_msg["content"]
