diff --git a/tests/test_finalizer_agent_runner.py b/tests/test_finalizer_agent_runner.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..ab055d91
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_finalizer_agent_runner.py
@@ -0,0 +1,147 @@
+"""Tests for finalizer_agent/agent_task_runner.py — task execution and result extraction."""
+
+import json
+from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+
+import pytest
+
+pytest.importorskip("hana_program_synthesis", reason="internal SAP package not available in local dev venv")
+
+from finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner import _adjust_resource_requirements, run
+from finalizer_agent.models import FinalizerTaskRequest
+
+
+def _request(**kwargs) -> FinalizerTaskRequest:
+    defaults: dict = {
+        "task_id": "task-uuid-1",
+        "task_text": "Run the finalizer skill",
+        "repo_url": "https://github.com/org/repo",
+        "repo_commitish": "abc123",
+        "config_repo_url": "https://github.com/org/config",
+        "config_repo_commitish": "main",
+        "config_mcp_paths": ["mcp/sq.json"],
+        "config_skill_paths": ["skills/sq/SKILL.md"],
+    }
+    defaults.update(kwargs)
+    return FinalizerTaskRequest(**defaults)
+
+
+def _patch_sdk(
+    task_id: str = "task-uuid-1",
+    completion_text: str = "Analysis complete.",
+    cline_state: dict | None = None,
+    mlflow_run_id: str = "run-xyz",
+):
+    """Return a context manager that patches pipeline3 and scheduling_doit."""
+    traj_dict = {"mlflow_run_id": mlflow_run_id}
+    if cline_state:
+        traj_dict["cline_state"] = json.dumps(cline_state)
+
+    mock_swe_task = MagicMock()
+    mock_pipeline3 = MagicMock()
+    mock_pipeline3.SoftwareEngineeringTask.return_value = mock_swe_task
+    mock_pipeline3.trigger_cline_central.return_value = {task_id: {"requirement_cpus_n": 4}}
+    mock_pipeline3.get_completion_text.return_value = {task_id: completion_text}
+
+    mock_scheduling_doit = MagicMock()
+    mock_scheduling_doit.schedule_tasks.return_value = {
+        task_id: {"atom_id": "atom-1", "atom_inspector_url": "https://atom.example.com/1"}
+    }
+    mock_scheduling_doit.collect_trajectories.return_value = {task_id: traj_dict}
+
+    return (
+        patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner.pipeline3", mock_pipeline3),
+        patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner.scheduling_doit", mock_scheduling_doit),
+        patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner._ensure_doit_configured"),
+    )
+
+
+class TestAdjustResourceRequirements:
+    def test_overrides_cpu_memory_disk(self) -> None:
+        """Resource requirements are reduced from defaults to finalizer-appropriate values."""
+        task: dict = {"requirement_cpus_n": 4, "requirement_mem_gib": 16, "requirement_disk_gib": 20}
+        _adjust_resource_requirements(task)
+        assert task["requirement_cpus_n"] == 1
+        assert task["requirement_mem_gib"] == 4
+        assert task["requirement_disk_gib"] == 8
+
+
+class TestRun:
+    def test_happy_path_full(self) -> None:
+        """run() returns populated result with mlflow_run_id and atom_inspector_url."""
+        cline_state = {"currentTaskItem": {"tokensIn": 500, "tokensOut": 200}}
+        p3_ctx, sched_ctx, doit_ctx = _patch_sdk(
+            completion_text="Fixed the issue.",
+            cline_state=cline_state,
+            mlflow_run_id="run-abc",
+        )
+        with p3_ctx, sched_ctx, doit_ctx:
+            result = run(_request())
+
+        assert result.task_id == "task-uuid-1"
+        assert result.completion_text == "Fixed the issue."
+        assert result.mlflow_run_id == "run-abc"
+        assert result.atom_inspector_url == "https://atom.example.com/1"
+        assert result.tokens_in == 500
+        assert result.tokens_out == 200
+
+    def test_raises_runtime_error_when_task_id_missing_from_trajectories(self) -> None:
+        """RuntimeError raised when task_id not present in collected trajectories."""
+        mock_p3 = MagicMock()
+        mock_p3.SoftwareEngineeringTask.return_value = MagicMock()
+        mock_p3.trigger_cline_central.return_value = {"task-uuid-1": {}}
+        mock_p3.get_completion_text.return_value = {}
+
+        mock_sched = MagicMock()
+        mock_sched.schedule_tasks.return_value = {"task-uuid-1": {}}
+        mock_sched.collect_trajectories.return_value = {}  # task_id absent
+
+        with (
+            patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner.pipeline3", mock_p3),
+            patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner.scheduling_doit", mock_sched),
+            patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner._ensure_doit_configured"),
+        ):
+            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="task-uuid-1"):
+                run(_request())
+
+    def test_token_extraction_failure_does_not_raise(self) -> None:
+        """Malformed cline_state is silently swallowed — result is still returned."""
+        p3_ctx, sched_ctx, doit_ctx = _patch_sdk(
+            completion_text="Done.",
+            cline_state={"currentTaskItem": {"tokensIn": "not-a-number"}},
+        )
+        with p3_ctx, sched_ctx, doit_ctx:
+            result = run(_request())
+
+        assert result.completion_text == "Done."
+        assert result.tokens_in is None
+
+    def test_resource_requirements_adjusted_for_all_tasks(self) -> None:
+        """_adjust_resource_requirements is applied to every task returned by trigger_cline_central."""
+        captured_tasks: dict = {}
+
+        def _fake_schedule(tasks_map, **kwargs):
+            captured_tasks.update(tasks_map)
+            return {"task-uuid-1": {"atom_id": "a1", "atom_inspector_url": None}}
+
+        mock_p3 = MagicMock()
+        mock_p3.SoftwareEngineeringTask.return_value = MagicMock()
+        mock_p3.trigger_cline_central.return_value = {
+            "task-uuid-1": {"requirement_cpus_n": 4, "requirement_mem_gib": 16, "requirement_disk_gib": 20}
+        }
+        mock_p3.get_completion_text.return_value = {"task-uuid-1": "done"}
+
+        mock_sched = MagicMock()
+        mock_sched.schedule_tasks.side_effect = _fake_schedule
+        mock_sched.collect_trajectories.return_value = {"task-uuid-1": {"mlflow_run_id": None}}
+
+        with (
+            patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner.pipeline3", mock_p3),
+            patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner.scheduling_doit", mock_sched),
+            patch("finalizer_agent.agent_task_runner._ensure_doit_configured"),
+        ):
+            run(_request())
+
+        task = captured_tasks["task-uuid-1"]
+        assert task["requirement_cpus_n"] == 1
+        assert task["requirement_mem_gib"] == 4
diff --git a/tests/test_jwks_client.py b/tests/test_jwks_client.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..15a3e49b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_jwks_client.py
@@ -0,0 +1,160 @@
+"""Tests for JwksClient — key fetching, cache TTL, and refresh logic."""
+
+import time
+from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch
+
+import pytest
+
+from fl_control_plane.auth_models import JwksUnavailableError
+from fl_control_plane.jwks_client import JwksClient
+
+
+def _jwk(kid: str) -> dict:
+    return {"kid": kid, "kty": "RSA", "n": "abc", "e": "AQAB"}
+
+
+def _mock_httpx_response(keys: list[dict], status: int = 200) -> MagicMock:
+    resp = MagicMock()
+    resp.raise_for_status = MagicMock()
+    resp.json = MagicMock(return_value={"keys": keys})
+    return resp
+
+
+class TestJwksClient:
+    def test_jwks_uri_property(self) -> None:
+        """jwks_uri property returns the URI passed at construction."""
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/oauth2/certs")
+        assert client.jwks_uri == "https://ias.example.com/oauth2/certs"
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_returns_key_after_refresh(self) -> None:
+        """get_key returns the key with matching kid after fetching from endpoint."""
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/jwks", ttl_seconds=600)
+        resp = _mock_httpx_response([_jwk("key-1")])
+
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.jwks_client.httpx.AsyncClient") as mock_cls:
+            mock_http = AsyncMock()
+            mock_http.get = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_http)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+
+            key = await client.get_key("key-1")
+
+        assert key["kid"] == "key-1"
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_raises_for_unknown_kid_after_refresh(self) -> None:
+        """JwksUnavailableError raised when kid not found even after refresh."""
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/jwks")
+        resp = _mock_httpx_response([_jwk("other-key")])
+
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.jwks_client.httpx.AsyncClient") as mock_cls:
+            mock_http = AsyncMock()
+            mock_http.get = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_http)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+
+            with pytest.raises(JwksUnavailableError, match="Unknown JWT signing key"):
+                await client.get_key("missing-key")
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_uses_cache_when_fresh(self) -> None:
+        """get_key does not re-fetch when cache is fresh and kid is known."""
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/jwks", ttl_seconds=600)
+        client._keys_by_kid = {"key-1": _jwk("key-1")}
+        client._fetched_at = time.monotonic()  # just fetched
+
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.jwks_client.httpx.AsyncClient") as mock_cls:
+            key = await client.get_key("key-1")
+            mock_cls.assert_not_called()  # no HTTP call made
+
+        assert key["kid"] == "key-1"
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_refreshes_when_stale(self) -> None:
+        """get_key re-fetches when TTL has expired."""
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/jwks", ttl_seconds=1)
+        client._keys_by_kid = {"key-1": _jwk("key-1")}
+        client._fetched_at = time.monotonic() - 100  # expired
+
+        resp = _mock_httpx_response([_jwk("key-1")])
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.jwks_client.httpx.AsyncClient") as mock_cls:
+            mock_http = AsyncMock()
+            mock_http.get = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_http)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+
+            await client.get_key("key-1")
+            mock_http.get.assert_called_once()
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_raises_on_http_error(self) -> None:
+        """JwksUnavailableError raised when JWKS endpoint returns HTTP error."""
+        import httpx
+
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/jwks")
+
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.jwks_client.httpx.AsyncClient") as mock_cls:
+            mock_http = AsyncMock()
+            mock_http.get = AsyncMock(side_effect=httpx.HTTPError("connection refused"))
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_http)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+
+            with pytest.raises(JwksUnavailableError, match="JWKS fetch failed"):
+                await client.get_key("key-1")
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_raises_on_missing_keys_array(self) -> None:
+        """JwksUnavailableError raised when response has no 'keys' array."""
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/jwks")
+        resp = MagicMock()
+        resp.raise_for_status = MagicMock()
+        resp.json = MagicMock(return_value={"not_keys": []})
+
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.jwks_client.httpx.AsyncClient") as mock_cls:
+            mock_http = AsyncMock()
+            mock_http.get = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_http)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+
+            with pytest.raises(JwksUnavailableError, match="missing 'keys' array"):
+                await client.get_key("key-1")
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_raises_on_invalid_json(self) -> None:
+        """JwksUnavailableError raised when endpoint returns invalid JSON."""
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/jwks")
+        resp = MagicMock()
+        resp.raise_for_status = MagicMock()
+        resp.json = MagicMock(side_effect=ValueError("No JSON object"))
+
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.jwks_client.httpx.AsyncClient") as mock_cls:
+            mock_http = AsyncMock()
+            mock_http.get = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_http)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+
+            with pytest.raises(JwksUnavailableError, match="not valid JSON"):
+                await client.get_key("key-1")
+
+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_keys_without_kid_are_ignored(self) -> None:
+        """Keys in the JWKS response without 'kid' field are silently dropped."""
+        client = JwksClient("https://ias.example.com/jwks")
+        resp = _mock_httpx_response(
+            [
+                {"kty": "RSA", "n": "x"},  # no kid
+                _jwk("valid-key"),
+            ]
+        )
+
+        with patch("fl_control_plane.jwks_client.httpx.AsyncClient") as mock_cls:
+            mock_http = AsyncMock()
+            mock_http.get = AsyncMock(return_value=resp)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aenter__ = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_http)
+            mock_cls.return_value.__aexit__ = AsyncMock(return_value=False)
+
+            key = await client.get_key("valid-key")
+
+        assert key["kid"] == "valid-key"
+        assert len(client._keys_by_kid) == 1

diff --git a/tests/test_models_and_repository.py b/tests/test_models_and_repository.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..7af372b5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_models_and_repository.py
@@ -0,0 +1,231 @@
+"""Tests for finalizer_agent models and execution_engine repository."""
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import ValidationError
+
+from finalizer_agent.models import (
+    FinalizerTaskRequest,
+    FinalizerTaskResult,
+    RawClineState,
+    RawClineTaskItem,
+    RawTrajectory,
+)
+from fl_control_plane.execution_engine.models import PipelineRun
+from fl_control_plane.execution_engine.repository import (
+    InMemoryHandlerRepository,
+    InMemoryPipelineRunRepository,
+)
+from fl_shared.cr_models import FaultHandlerSpec
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# FinalizerTaskRequest
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestFinalizerTaskRequest:
+    def _valid(self, **kwargs) -> dict:
+        base: dict = {
+            "task_id": "task-uuid-1",
+            "task_text": "Run the finalizer",
+            "repo_url": "https://github.com/org/repo",
+            "repo_commitish": "abc123",
+            "config_repo_url": "https://github.com/org/config",
+            "config_repo_commitish": "main",
+            "config_mcp_paths": ["mcp/sq.json"],
+            "config_skill_paths": ["skills/sq/SKILL.md"],
+        }
+        base.update(kwargs)
+        return base
+
+    def test_valid_construction(self) -> None:
+        """All required fields set constructs successfully."""
+        req = FinalizerTaskRequest(**self._valid())
+        assert req.task_id == "task-uuid-1"
+        assert req.config_mcp_paths == ["mcp/sq.json"]
+
+    def test_missing_required_field_raises(self) -> None:
+        """Missing required field raises ValidationError."""
+        data = self._valid()
+        del data["task_id"]
+        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
+            FinalizerTaskRequest(**data)
+
+    def test_empty_lists_accepted(self) -> None:
+        """Empty config_mcp_paths and config_skill_paths are valid."""
+        req = FinalizerTaskRequest(**self._valid(config_mcp_paths=[], config_skill_paths=[]))
+        assert req.config_mcp_paths == []
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# FinalizerTaskResult
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestFinalizerTaskResult:
+    def test_minimal_construction(self) -> None:
+        """Only task_id required; all other fields default to None."""
+        result = FinalizerTaskResult(task_id="t1")
+        assert result.task_id == "t1"
+        assert result.completion_text is None
+        assert result.tokens_in is None
+        assert result.tokens_out is None
+        assert result.mlflow_run_id is None
+        assert result.atom_inspector_url is None
+
+    def test_full_construction(self) -> None:
+        """All optional fields are stored correctly."""
+        result = FinalizerTaskResult(
+            task_id="t2",
+            completion_text="Done",
+            tokens_in=100,
+            tokens_out=50,
+            mlflow_run_id="run-123",
+            atom_inspector_url="https://atom.example.com/run-123",
+        )
+        assert result.completion_text == "Done"
+        assert result.tokens_in == 100
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# RawClineTaskItem — alias handling
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestRawClineTaskItem:
+    def test_parses_camel_case_aliases(self) -> None:
+        """tokensIn / tokensOut camelCase aliases are accepted."""
+        item = RawClineTaskItem.model_validate({"tokensIn": 200, "tokensOut": 80})
+        assert item.tokens_in == 200
+        assert item.tokens_out == 80
+
+    def test_parses_snake_case_field_names(self) -> None:
+        """Snake-case field names are accepted via populate_by_name=True."""
+        item = RawClineTaskItem.model_validate({"tokens_in": 10, "tokens_out": 5})
+        assert item.tokens_in == 10
+
+    def test_none_defaults(self) -> None:
+        """All fields default to None when omitted."""
+        item = RawClineTaskItem.model_validate({})
+        assert item.tokens_in is None
+        assert item.tokens_out is None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# RawClineState
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestRawClineState:
+    def test_parses_current_task_item_alias(self) -> None:
+        """currentTaskItem camelCase alias is parsed correctly."""
+        state = RawClineState.model_validate({"currentTaskItem": {"tokensIn": 300, "tokensOut": 150}})
+        assert state.current_task_item is not None
+        assert state.current_task_item.tokens_in == 300
+
+    def test_none_when_omitted(self) -> None:
+        """current_task_item is None when not in payload."""
+        state = RawClineState.model_validate({})
+        assert state.current_task_item is None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# RawTrajectory.parse_cline_state
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestRawTrajectory:
+    def test_parses_cline_state_json(self) -> None:
+        """parse_cline_state deserialises the JSON string."""
+        traj = RawTrajectory(
+            cline_state='{"currentTaskItem": {"tokensIn": 42, "tokensOut": 21}}',
+            mlflow_run_id="run-1",
+        )
+        state = traj.parse_cline_state()
+        assert state.current_task_item is not None
+        assert state.current_task_item.tokens_in == 42
+
+    def test_returns_empty_state_when_none(self) -> None:
+        """parse_cline_state returns empty RawClineState when cline_state is None."""
+        traj = RawTrajectory(cline_state=None)
+        state = traj.parse_cline_state()
+        assert state.current_task_item is None
+
+    def test_mlflow_run_id_stored(self) -> None:
+        """mlflow_run_id field is accessible."""
+        traj = RawTrajectory(mlflow_run_id="run-xyz")
+        assert traj.mlflow_run_id == "run-xyz"
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# InMemoryPipelineRunRepository
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _pipeline_run() -> PipelineRun:
+    return PipelineRun.model_validate(
+        {
+            "inspection_id": "insp-1",
+            "ci_system": "jenkins",
+            "repo_url": "https://github.com/org/repo",
+            "repo_commitish": "abc123",
+            "jenkins_url": "https://jenkins.example.com/job/x/1/",
+        }
+    )
+
+
+class TestInMemoryPipelineRunRepository:
+    def test_save_and_get(self) -> None:
+        """Saved run is retrievable by inspection_id."""
+        repo = InMemoryPipelineRunRepository()
+        run = _pipeline_run()
+        repo.save("insp-1", run)
+        result = repo.get("insp-1")
+        assert result is run
+
+    def test_get_returns_none_for_unknown(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None for an inspection_id that was never saved."""
+        repo = InMemoryPipelineRunRepository()
+        assert repo.get("unknown") is None
+
+    def test_overwrite_updates_value(self) -> None:
+        """Saving a second run under the same key replaces the first."""
+        repo = InMemoryPipelineRunRepository()
+        run1 = _pipeline_run()
+        run2 = _pipeline_run()
+        repo.save("insp-1", run1)
+        repo.save("insp-1", run2)
+        assert repo.get("insp-1") is run2
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# InMemoryHandlerRepository
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _handler_spec() -> FaultHandlerSpec:
+    return FaultHandlerSpec.model_validate(
+        {
+            "fault_id": "sonarqube-failure",
+            "execution": {"type": "agent_task", "task": "fix it"},
+        }
+    )
+
+
+class TestInMemoryHandlerRepository:
+    def test_save_and_get(self) -> None:
+        """Saved handler list is retrievable."""
+        repo = InMemoryHandlerRepository()
+        handlers = [_handler_spec()]
+        repo.save("insp-1", handlers)
+        assert repo.get("insp-1") == handlers
+
+    def test_get_returns_none_for_unknown(self) -> None:
+        """Returns None for unknown inspection_id."""
+        repo = InMemoryHandlerRepository()
+        assert repo.get("missing") is None
+
+    def test_save_empty_list(self) -> None:
+        """Empty handler list is saved and retrieved correctly."""
+        repo = InMemoryHandlerRepository()
+        repo.save("insp-1", [])
+        assert repo.get("insp-1") == []

diff --git a/tests/utils/test_github_client_full.py b/tests/utils/test_github_client_full.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..824c1c95
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/utils/test_github_client_full.py
@@ -0,0 +1,322 @@
+"""Tests for GithubClient methods — token resolution, body compression, comment sampling, timeline filtering."""
+
+import re
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+
+import pytest
+
+from fl_shared.utils.github_client import GithubClient, parse_general_github_url
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# parse_general_github_url
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestParseGeneralGithubUrl:
+    def test_parses_pull_request_url(self) -> None:
+        """Returns hostname, org, repo, pull number for a well-formed PR URL."""
+        hostname, org, repo, pull_no = parse_general_github_url("https://github.wdf.sap.corp/my-org/my-repo/pull/42")
+        assert hostname == "github.wdf.sap.corp"
+        assert org == "my-org"
+        assert repo == "my-repo"
+        assert pull_no == 42
+
+    def test_strips_git_suffix_from_repo(self) -> None:
+        """Trailing .git is stripped from the repo name."""
+        _, _, repo, _ = parse_general_github_url("https://github.com/org/repo.git")
+        assert repo == "repo"
+
+    def test_pull_no_none_for_non_numeric(self) -> None:
+        """pull_no is None when the value after /pull/ is not an integer."""
+        _, _, _, pull_no = parse_general_github_url("https://github.com/org/repo/pull/abc")
+        assert pull_no is None
+
+    def test_pull_no_none_when_no_pull_in_path(self) -> None:
+        """pull_no is None when URL has no /pull/ segment."""
+        _, _, _, pull_no = parse_general_github_url("https://github.com/org/repo/issues/5")
+        assert pull_no is None
+
+    def test_all_none_for_bare_hostname(self) -> None:
+        """org, repo, pull_no are None for a bare hostname URL."""
+        hostname, org, repo, pull_no = parse_general_github_url("https://github.com")
+        assert hostname == "github.com"
+        assert org is None
+        assert repo is None
+        assert pull_no is None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# GithubClient — token resolution
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _make_client(hostname: str = "github.example.com", token: str = "tok") -> GithubClient:
+    """Build a GithubClient with a patched settings object."""
+    fake_settings = SimpleNamespace(
+        github_wdf_token=None,
+        github_tool_token=None,
+        ssl_ca_bundle=None,
+    )
+    with patch("fl_shared.utils.github_client.settings", fake_settings):
+        return GithubClient(hostname, "org", "repo", token=token)
+
+
+class TestGithubClientTokenResolution:
+    def test_explicit_token_used_directly(self) -> None:
+        """When explicit token is provided, it is used regardless of settings."""
+        fake_settings = SimpleNamespace(github_wdf_token=None, github_tool_token=None, ssl_ca_bundle=None)
+        with patch("fl_shared.utils.github_client.settings", fake_settings):
+            client = GithubClient("github.example.com", "org", "repo", token="explicit-tok")
+        assert "explicit-tok" in client._client.headers.get("authorization", "")
+
+    def test_wdf_token_used_for_wdf_hostname(self) -> None:
+        """github_wdf_token is used when hostname contains 'wdf'."""
+        wdf_secret = MagicMock()
+        wdf_secret.get_secret_value.return_value = "wdf-secret-tok"
+        fake_settings = SimpleNamespace(github_wdf_token=wdf_secret, github_tool_token=None, ssl_ca_bundle=None)
+        with patch("fl_shared.utils.github_client.settings", fake_settings):
+            client = GithubClient("github.wdf.sap.corp", "org", "repo")
+        assert "wdf-secret-tok" in client._client.headers.get("authorization", "")
+
+    def test_tool_token_used_for_non_wdf_hostname(self) -> None:
+        """github_tool_token is used when hostname does not contain 'wdf'."""
+        tool_secret = MagicMock()
+        tool_secret.get_secret_value.return_value = "tool-secret-tok"
+        fake_settings = SimpleNamespace(github_wdf_token=None, github_tool_token=tool_secret, ssl_ca_bundle=None)
+        with patch("fl_shared.utils.github_client.settings", fake_settings):
+            client = GithubClient("github.tools.sap.corp", "org", "repo")
+        assert "tool-secret-tok" in client._client.headers.get("authorization", "")
+
+    def test_raises_when_no_token_available(self) -> None:
+        """ValueError raised when neither explicit nor settings token is available."""
+        fake_settings = SimpleNamespace(github_wdf_token=None, github_tool_token=None, ssl_ca_bundle=None)
+        with patch("fl_shared.utils.github_client.settings", fake_settings):
+            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="GITHUB_WDF_TOKEN"):
+                GithubClient("github.example.com", "org", "repo")
+
+    def test_pull_no_setter_clears_cached_data(self) -> None:
+        """Setting pull_no clears the cached _pull_data."""
+        client = _make_client()
+        client._pull_data = {"title": "old"}
+        client.pull_no = 5
+        assert client._pull_data is None
+        assert client.pull_no == 5
+
+    def test_pull_data_property_raises(self) -> None:
+        """pull_data property raises AttributeError directing to async method."""
+        client = _make_client()
+        with pytest.raises(AttributeError, match="async"):
+            _ = client.pull_data
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# compress_pr_body
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestCompressPrBody:  # pylint: disable=attribute-defined-outside-init
+    def setup_method(self) -> None:
+        self.client = _make_client()
+
+    def test_empty_body_returns_defaults(self) -> None:
+        """Empty body returns dict with empty/zero values."""
+        result = self.client.compress_pr_body("")
+        assert result["first_paragraph"] == ""
+        assert not result["keywords"]
+        assert result["checkboxes"]["total"] == 0
+        assert not result["sections"]
+
+    def test_first_paragraph_stops_at_blank_line(self) -> None:
+        """First paragraph ends at first blank line."""
+        body = "Line one\nLine two\n\nLine three"
+        result = self.client.compress_pr_body(body)
+        assert "Line three" not in result["first_paragraph"]
+        assert "Line one" in result["first_paragraph"]
+
+    def test_first_paragraph_stops_at_heading(self) -> None:
+        """First paragraph ends at a markdown heading."""
+        body = "Intro text\n## Section\nMore text"
+        result = self.client.compress_pr_body(body)
+        assert "More text" not in result["first_paragraph"]
+
+    def test_keyword_matching_is_case_insensitive(self) -> None:
+        """Keywords are matched case-insensitively in the body."""
+        result = self.client.compress_pr_body("this PR is WIP", keywords=["wip"])
+        assert "wip" in result["keywords"]
+
+    def test_checkboxes_counted_correctly(self) -> None:
+        """Done and total checkbox counts are accurate."""
+        body = "- [x] Done item\n- [ ] Open item\n- [X] Also done"
+        result = self.client.compress_pr_body(body)
+        assert result["checkboxes"]["total"] == 3
+        assert result["checkboxes"]["done"] == 2
+        assert result["checkboxes"]["open_items"] == ["Open item"]
+
+    def test_section_extracted_by_heading(self) -> None:
+        """TODO section content is extracted under its key."""
+        body = "## TODO\nFix the tests\n## Notes\nOther stuff"
+        result = self.client.compress_pr_body(body)
+        assert "TODO" in result["sections"]
+        assert "Fix the tests" in result["sections"]["TODO"]
+
+    def test_no_keywords_when_none_passed(self) -> None:
+        """Empty keywords list when no keyword list provided."""
+        result = self.client.compress_pr_body("wip draft do not merge")
+        assert not result["keywords"]
+
+    def test_asterisk_bullets_counted_as_checkboxes(self) -> None:
+        """Both - and * bullet styles are recognised as checkboxes."""
+        body = "* [x] done\n* [ ] open"
+        result = self.client.compress_pr_body(body)
+        assert result["checkboxes"]["total"] == 2
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# sample_comments
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _comment(cid: int, login: str, body: str, created_at: str = "2024-01-01T00:00:00Z") -> dict:
+    return {"id": cid, "user": {"login": login}, "body": body, "created_at": created_at}
+
+
+class TestSampleComments:  # pylint: disable=attribute-defined-outside-init
+    def setup_method(self) -> None:
+        self.client = _make_client()
+
+    def test_empty_list_returns_empty(self) -> None:
+        """Empty input returns empty list."""
+        assert not self.client.sample_comments([], "author")
+
+    def test_raises_for_non_list_input(self) -> None:
+        """TypeError raised when comments argument is not a list."""
+        with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="Expected list"):
+            self.client.sample_comments("not a list", "author")  # type: ignore[arg-type]
+
+    def test_selects_most_recent_comments(self) -> None:
+        """Returns the last max_recent comments when list exceeds limit."""
+        comments = [_comment(i, "user", f"msg {i}", f"2024-01-{i:02d}T00:00:00Z") for i in range(1, 15)]
+        result = self.client.sample_comments(comments, "other", max_recent=5)
+        assert len(result) <= 6  # 5 recent + possible author addition
+
+    def test_includes_last_pr_author_comment(self) -> None:
+        """PR author's most recent comment is included even if not in recent window."""
+        author_comment = _comment(1, "alice", "author comment", "2024-01-01T00:00:00Z")
+        recent_comments = [_comment(i, "bob", f"msg {i}", f"2024-01-{i:02d}T00:00:00Z") for i in range(2, 12)]
+        comments = [author_comment, *recent_comments]
+        result = self.client.sample_comments(comments, "alice", max_recent=5)
+        authors = {r["author"] for r in result}
+        assert "alice" in authors
+
+    def test_keyword_matching_adds_to_result(self) -> None:
+        """Comments matching keyword pattern are included in result."""
+        comments = [
+            _comment(1, "user", "WIP: still working on it"),
+            _comment(2, "user", "looks good to me"),
+            _comment(3, "user", "LGTM"),
+        ]
+        pattern = re.compile(r"\bwip\b", re.IGNORECASE)
+        result = self.client.sample_comments(comments, "other", keyword_pattern=pattern, max_recent=1)
+        bodies = [r["body"] for r in result]
+        assert any("WIP" in b for b in bodies)
+
+    def test_code_blocks_without_errors_removed(self) -> None:
+        """Non-error code blocks are replaced with placeholder."""
+        comments = [_comment(1, "user", "Here:\n```\nsome code\n```\nDone")]
+        result = self.client.sample_comments(comments, "other")
+        assert "[code block removed]" in result[0]["body"]
+
+    def test_code_blocks_with_errors_preserved(self) -> None:
+        """Code blocks containing Error/Exception are kept."""
+        comments = [_comment(1, "user", "```\nAssertionError: wrong\n```")]
+        result = self.client.sample_comments(comments, "other")
+        assert "AssertionError" in result[0]["body"]
+
+    def test_has_wip_keywords_field_added_with_pattern(self) -> None:
+        """has_wip_keywords field is added to each result when keyword_pattern provided."""
+        comments = [_comment(1, "user", "some comment")]
+        pattern = re.compile(r"wip", re.IGNORECASE)
+        result = self.client.sample_comments(comments, "other", keyword_pattern=pattern)
+        assert "has_wip_keywords" in result[0]
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# filter_timeline_event
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class TestFilterTimelineEvent:  # pylint: disable=attribute-defined-outside-init
+    def setup_method(self) -> None:
+        self.client = _make_client()
+
+    def test_committed_event_extracts_sha_and_message(self) -> None:
+        """committed events include sha (first 8 chars), message, author, date."""
+        event = {
+            "event": "committed",
+            "sha": "abcdef1234567890",
+            "message": "fix: resolve the issue",
+            "author": {"name": "Alice", "date": "2024-01-15T10:00:00Z"},
+        }
+        result = self.client.filter_timeline_event(event)
+        assert result["sha"] == "abcdef12"
+        assert result["message"] == "fix: resolve the issue"
+        assert result["author"] == "Alice"
+
+    def test_ready_for_review_event(self) -> None:
+        """ready_for_review extracts created_at and actor login."""
+        event = {
+            "event": "ready_for_review",
+            "created_at": "2024-01-10T09:00:00Z",
+            "actor": {"login": "bob"},
+        }
+        result = self.client.filter_timeline_event(event)
+        assert result["created_at"] == "2024-01-10T09:00:00Z"
+        assert result["actor"] == "bob"
+
+    def test_convert_to_draft_event(self) -> None:
+        """convert_to_draft extracts created_at and actor login."""
+        event = {"event": "convert_to_draft", "created_at": "2024-01-05T08:00:00Z", "actor": {"login": "carol"}}
+        result = self.client.filter_timeline_event(event)
+        assert result["actor"] == "carol"
+
+    def test_labeled_event_extracts_label_name(self) -> None:
+        """labeled events include label name."""
+        event = {
+            "event": "labeled",
+            "created_at": "2024-01-12T00:00:00Z",
+            "actor": {"login": "dan"},
+            "label": {"name": "wip"},
+        }
+        result = self.client.filter_timeline_event(event)
+        assert result["label"] == "wip"
+
+    def test_unlabeled_event(self) -> None:
+        """unlabeled events also include label name."""
+        event = {"event": "unlabeled", "created_at": "x", "actor": {"login": "e"}, "label": {"name": "done"}}
+        result = self.client.filter_timeline_event(event)
+        assert result["label"] == "done"
+
+    def test_commented_event_extracts_body(self) -> None:
+        """commented events include truncated body."""
+        body = "A" * 500
+        event = {"event": "commented", "created_at": "x", "actor": {"login": "f"}, "body": body}
+        result = self.client.filter_timeline_event(event)
+        assert len(result["body"]) <= 300
+
+    def test_review_requested_event(self) -> None:
+        """review_requested includes requested_reviewer login."""
+        event = {
+            "event": "review_requested",
+            "created_at": "x",
+            "actor": {"login": "g"},
+            "requested_reviewer": {"login": "h"},
+        }
+        result = self.client.filter_timeline_event(event)
+        assert result["requested_reviewer"] == "h"
+
+    def test_unknown_event_type_returns_base_fields_only(self) -> None:
+        """Unknown event types return event type, created_at, and actor only."""
+        event = {"event": "subscribed", "created_at": "x", "actor": {"login": "i"}}
+        result = self.client.filter_timeline_event(event)
+        assert set(result.keys()) == {"event", "created_at", "actor"}
